<p align="center">
    <strong>🏆 A ranked list of the best open-source tools for reading markets and the world with AI — crypto, trading, tokenomics, macro, geopolitics, and the agents/skills/MCP that connect them.</strong>
</p>

<p align="center">
    <a href="#contents"><img src="https://img.shields.io/static/v1?label=%F0%9F%8F%86&message=tools&color=5470c6"></a>
    <a href="https://github.com/best-of-lists/best-of/blob/main/contribution.md#best-of-badge"><img src="http://bit.ly/3rdrmZh"></a>
</p>

This curated list ranks projects by a **project-quality score** computed automatically from GitHub signals (stars, activity, contributors, age, last commit). **Rankings refresh every week** via GitHub Actions — dead and stale projects sink automatically, so the list never rots.

> **Why this list is different.** Most awesome-lists are hand-maintained snapshots that go stale within months. This one is **discovered by metadata _and_ by code** (so tools without the right GitHub topic still get found) and **re-scored weekly** from live signals. Health and activity are visible on every entry.

> 🔎 **Looking for the few I actually use, with methodology?** This is the wide net — _every tool we see value in_. The hand-picked, epistemically-vetted shortlist lives separately as **Emergence Picks** _(link coming)_.

🧙‍♂️ **Discover other best-of lists** at [best-of.org](https://best-of.org) · 📲 **Suggest a tool** by opening a pull request (see [Contribution](#contribution)).

## Contents

- [Crypto Trading Bots & Frameworks](#crypto-trading-bots--frameworks) _344 projects_
- [Backtesting & Quant Frameworks](#backtesting--quant-frameworks) _916 projects_
- [Market & Exchange Data](#market--exchange-data) _887 projects_
- [DeFi & Tokenomics Analytics](#defi--tokenomics-analytics) _42 projects_
- [On-Chain & Wallet Analytics](#on-chain--wallet-analytics) _108 projects_
- [Fundamentals & SEC Filings](#fundamentals--sec-filings) _84 projects_
- [Macro, Geopolitics & World Intelligence](#macro-geopolitics--world-intelligence) _83 projects_
- [AI Agents, Skills & MCP for Markets](#ai-agents-skills--mcp-for-markets) _372 projects_
- [Dashboards, Terminals & Financial Data](#dashboards-terminals--financial-data) _229 projects_
- [Research, Lists & Discovery](#research-lists--discovery) _119 projects_

## Explanation
- 🥇🥈🥉&nbsp; Combined project-quality score
- ⭐️&nbsp; Star count from GitHub
- 🐣&nbsp; New project _(less than 6 months old)_
- 💤&nbsp; Inactive project _(6 months no activity)_
- 💀&nbsp; Dead project _(12 months no activity)_
- 📈📉&nbsp; Project is trending up or down
- ➕&nbsp; Project was recently added
- ❗️&nbsp; Warning _(e.g. missing/risky license)_
- 👨‍💻&nbsp; Contributors count from GitHub
- 🔀&nbsp; Fork count from GitHub
- 📋&nbsp; Issue count from GitHub
- ⏱️&nbsp; Last update timestamp on package manager
- 📥&nbsp; Download count from package manager
- 📦&nbsp; Number of dependent projects

<br>

## Contribution

Contributions are encouraged and always welcome! 🙏

- To add a tool, open a pull request that edits **`projects.yaml`** — add an entry with at least a `github_id` and a `category`. Do **not** edit `README.md` directly; it is generated automatically.
- To report a stale, dead, or miscategorized project, open an issue.
- No pull request needed: [suggest a tool](https://github.com/arturoabreuhd/best-of-ai-markets/issues/new?template=suggest-tool.yml) with the issue form.
- To flag something for the curated **Emergence Picks** shortlist, label your PR `emergence-candidate`.

A project is judged on its open-source merit, activity, and usefulness — not popularity alone. Low-star but genuinely useful tools in sparse domains (tokenomics, geopolitical risk) are included on purpose and clearly framed.

## License

Tool metadata is sourced from public GitHub data. Each listed project keeps its own license. This list is released under [CC-BY-SA-4.0](https://creativecommons.org/licenses/by-sa/4.0/).
